Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on computer_vision structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 449. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 926 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to computer_vision theory, we decompose the problem into 449 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 926. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
